With a bounded bank of retained observations, it streams long videos at constant memory and remains more faithful than full softmax under the same budget.

</details>


### [175] [EviRover: Reinforcing Agentic Perception Beyond a Glance](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.40230)
*Kaixuan Fan,Kaituo Feng,Tianshuo Peng,Yilei Jiang,Manyuan Zhang,Junke Wang,Xiangyu Yue*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 论文提出"证据不足下的感知"问题，构建训练数据与基准，训练出首个交互式感知智能体EviRover。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统视觉感知假设单次看图即可回答，但现实中很多查询依赖细粒度细节或需知识密集、最新信息，单次预测不足以解决。

Method: 设计两条数据生成流程得到EviRover-SFT-5K与EviRover-RL-12K；构建人工校验基准EviLens（688例、5类）；用监督微调加智能体强化学习训练EviRover。

Result: 4B的EviRover在EviLens上平均超基线30分，媲美先进闭源模型；效果迁移至WebEyes、常规感知与通用多模态基准，BrowseComp-VL提升15分。

Conclusion: 感知可被建模为可交互获取额外信息的智能体过程，交互式训练能显著提升感知能力并具备良好泛化性，代码模型数据全部开源。

Abstract: Visual perception is conventionally formulated as a one-shot prediction from a single glance at the image, under the assumption that the image content and the model's parametric knowledge suffice to resolve the query. This assumption often fails in real-world scenarios that hinge on fine-grained visual details or require knowledge-intensive and up-to-date information. We term such cases \textit{perception under insufficient evidence} and formulate perception as an agentic process that can obtain information beyond a single glance. To address the absence of data for this setting, we design two dedicated data generation pipelines, yielding EviRover-SFT-5K and EviRover-RL-12K for training. We further construct EviLens, a human-verified benchmark comprising 688 instances across five perception categories. Building on these data, we present EviRover, to our knowledge the first perception agent explicitly trained to resolve perceptual queries through interaction, using supervised fine-tuning followed by agentic reinforcement learning. Experiments show that the 4B EviRover outperforms its backbone by 30 points on average on EviLens, reaching performance comparable to advanced proprietary models. The gains transfer beyond EviLens to WebEyes, conventional perception benchmarks, and general multimodal benchmarks, including a 15-point improvement on BrowseComp-VL. All code, models, and data are released.

</details>


### [176] [StreamRig: Exploiting Intra-Rig Geometry for Streaming Multi-Camera Odometry](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.40244)
*Yufei Wei,Shuhao Ye,Qi Wang,Xin Zheng,Qing Huang,Rong Xiong,Yue Wang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: StreamRig 是一个基于冻结多视角3D基础模型的因果流式里程计框架，仅训练74.6M参数即可在标定相机组上实现高效稳定的位姿估计。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 移动机器人与车辆常搭载同步多相机组，但许多流式3D基础模型仅面向单目输入，如何高效利用相机组几何信息仍是挑战。

Method: 采用"冻结-流式"框架：冻结前端利用标定联合感知同步视角，Rig-Resampler压缩特征，CausalBridge使用带KV缓存的因果注意力，轻量头回归位姿，并辅以周期性重锚定协议；仅训练这些模块（共74.6M参数），以相对位姿为唯一监督，并采用分组重定位预训练与因果相机组训练的两阶段策略。

Result: 在NCLT、TartanGround、KITTI-360及自采人形机器人数据集ZJH上，训练仅用仿真数据、真实世界为零样本评估，StreamRig在平移和旋转漂移上均优于所评估的非oracle单目流式模型与相机组感知离线模型，且推理成本低；消融与相机数量控制实验揭示了增益来源。

Conclusion: StreamRig可高效利用相机组几何，在冻结基础模型上实现稳定的长序列因果流式里程计，并已开源。

Abstract: Mobile robots and vehicles carry synchronized multi-camera rigs, yet many streaming 3D foundation models are designed for monocular input, leaving efficient use of rig geometry a challenge. We present StreamRig, a freeze-and-stream framework that builds causal streaming odometry for calibrated rigs on a frozen multi-view 3D foundation model. The frozen front-end jointly perceives the synchronized views using rig calibration. A Rig-Resampler compresses their features, a CausalBridge applies causal attention with a key-value cache, and a lightweight head regresses rig poses. A periodic re-anchoring protocol supports stable pose estimation over long sequences. Only these modules are trained, 74.6M parameters in total, with relative poses as the sole supervision. Our two-stage training strategy combines group relocalization pretraining with causal rig training to transfer the geometric priors of the frozen front-end and the alignment ability of the pretrained modules to streaming odometry. We evaluate on NCLT, TartanGround, KITTI-360, and our self-collected humanoid-robot dataset ZJH, where training uses only simulation and real-world evaluation is zero-shot. Across all four datasets, StreamRig achieves lower translation and rotation drift than the evaluated non-oracle monocular streaming and rig-aware offline models, while maintaining low inference cost. Ablations and controlled camera-count experiments identify the sources of these gains. We further examine how longer training windows affect inference over longer horizons. Code has been released at https://github.com/WeiYuFei0217/StreamRig.

</details>


### [177] [ComputerSD: Online Self-Distillation from Real-Time Feedback for Computer-Use Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.40253)
*Yong Du,Tongbo Chen,Zhengxi Lu,Yizhou Liu,Bofan Chen,Tao Jiang,Wenhao Xu,Yongliang Shen*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出 ComputerSD，一种面向计算机使用智能体（CUA）的在线自蒸馏方法，将GUI转换的实时反馈转化为策略学习指导，联合优化token级OPSD与轨迹级GRPO。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有CUA在线训练主要依赖稀疏的结果奖励，无法为中间动作提供监督。在线策略自蒸馏（OPSD）虽能提供token级学习信号，但直接应用于CUA在线训练存在两个问题：固定的指导可能与学生的当前状态不匹配；指导导致的概率偏移可能与步骤级正确性冲突。

Method: ComputerSD 通过微调的GUI分析器在执行每个动作后生成指导（提供特权上下文）和步骤级价值分数（用于调节OPSD信号），并将token级OPSD与轨迹级GRPO在完全异步的训练框架中联合优化。

Result: 在 OSWorld-Verified 上，ComputerSD 相比仅使用结果奖励的GRPO，在通用 Qwen3-VL-8B-Thinking 和专用 EvoCUA-8B 骨干上分别提升 1.9 和 4.1 个百分点；分布外评估进一步支持其泛化性。

Conclusion: 结果表明，通过在线自蒸馏从实时反馈中学习对计算机使用智能体是有效的。

Abstract: Online training enables computer-use agents (CUAs) to improve through interaction with executable environments. However, existing methods primarily rely on sparse outcome rewards, which provide no supervision for intermediate actions. On-policy self-distillation (OPSD) offers token-level learning signals through privileged rescoring, but directly applying it to CUA online training presents two challenges: fixed guidance may become misaligned with the student's current state, and guidance-induced probability shifts may conflict with step-level correctness. We introduce ComputerSD, an online self-distillation method for CUAs that converts real-time feedback from executed GUI transitions into guidance for policy learning. A fine-tuned GUI analyzer produces guidance and a step-level value score after each action; the guidance provides privileged context, while the score regulates the resulting OPSD signals. ComputerSD jointly optimizes token-level OPSD and trajectory-level GRPO in a fully asynchronous training framework. On OSWorld-Verified, ComputerSD outperforms outcome-only GRPO by 1.9 and 4.1 percentage points on the general-purpose Qwen3-VL-8B-Thinking and specialized EvoCUA-8B backbones, respectively. Evaluation in out-of-distribution settings further supports the generalizability of ComputerSD. These results demonstrate the effectiveness of learning from real-time feedback through online self-distillation for CUAs.

</details>


### [178] [Looped Diffusion Transformer](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.40305)
*Yong Xien Chng,Tianyi Chen,Wenwen Tong,Haiwen Diao,Zhongang Cai,Lei Yang,Ziwei Liu,Lewei Lu,Dahua Lin,Gao Huang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出Looped Diffusion Transformer (Looped-DiT)，通过在每个去噪步骤内重复运行共享Transformer块来增加计算深度而保持参数量不变，结合中间循环深监督与自调制注意力，实现更高效的视觉生成扩展。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统提升文生图模型依赖增大模型规模或增加去噪步数。作者希望探索一种替代方式，即在不增加参数量的前提下通过重复使用共享Transformer块来扩展计算深度。然而朴素循环无法稳定提升图像质量，原因是中间循环监督不足以及注意力更新不受约束逐渐侵蚀局部信息。

Method: 提出Looped-DiT，将循环计算与以下技术结合：跨中间循环的深监督（deep supervision across intermediate loops），以及自调制注意力（self-modulating attention），用以稳定循环特征更新。

Result: 在匹配参数和匹配计算设置下，Looped-DiT持续优于非循环基线。一个260M参数的循环模型在多个文生图基准上超越大6.5倍的模型，同时推理计算量低4.9倍。固定推理预算下，增加循环深度比增加去噪步数收益更大；更深循环能逐步纠正在早期循环中的错误，表现出潜在推理行为。

Conclusion: 循环计算为扩散模型提供了更有效的迭代计算形式，能够逐步纠正早期错误并展现潜在推理特性，是扩展视觉生成模型的一种有前景的方式。

Abstract: Improving text-to-image models has traditionally relied on increasing model size or the number of denoising steps. In this work, we explore an alternative way to scale computation by repeatedly running shared Transformer blocks within each denoising step, effectively increasing computational depth while keeping the parameter count fixed. This looped computation enables iterative refinement of internal representations without explicit reasoning tokens. However, naive looping fails to consistently improve image quality. We trace this problem to weak supervision across intermediate loops and unregulated attention updates that progressively erode local information. To overcome these challenges, we propose Looped Diffusion Transformer (Looped-DiT), which combines deep supervision across intermediate loops with self-modulating attention to stabilize looped feature updates. Under matched-parameter and matched-compute settings, Looped-DiT consistently outperforms non-looped baselines. Notably, a 260M-parameter looped model can surpass a model 6.5x larger across multiple text-to-image benchmarks while requiring 4.9x lower inference compute. Beyond this performance gain, we find that looped computation can offer a more effective form of iterative computation for diffusion models, with increasing loop depth yielding larger gains than adding more denoising steps under a fixed inference budget. Furthermore, deeper loops can progressively correct mistakes made in earlier loops, exhibiting behaviors suggestive of latent reasoning. Together, these results show that looped computation offers a promising way to scale visual generation models.

</details>


### [179] [GLARE: Generating Listening Heads with Appropriate Reactions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.40317)
*Zikai Liao,Yumin Suh,Yi Ouyang,Yi-Lun Lee,Yi-Hsuan Tsai,Zhaozheng Yin*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文针对对话中听者行为生成的数据、建模与评估三大缺口，构建了细粒度听者反应数据集，提出基于流匹配Transformer的音频驱动基线GLARE，并设计了面向反应的评估协议，在视觉与反应指标上均优于以往方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有说话头生成进展迅速，但双人对话中听者行为（何时反应、如何反应、以何种类型反应）的自然生成仍被忽视。已有双人数据集缺乏细粒度听者反应标注，且沿用说话头与视频生成的评估指标只衡量视觉真实感，无法反映听者反应是否恰当。

Method: 从三个方面解决：(1) 基于RealTalk与Seamless Interaction构建听者头部专用数据集，约147小时成对说话人-听者视频，含64,557条事件级反应标注，覆盖点头、摇头、微笑、大笑、皱眉、惊讶六类；(2) 提出音频驱动基线GLARE，基于流匹配Transformer，采用Qwen2-Audio提取韵律条件，并引入时间反应损失显式监督逐帧反应；(3) 提出面向反应的评估协议，联合度量反应发生（R-F1）、时间对齐（R-tIoU）、非对称时间偏差（R-ATD）与反应区域视觉质量（R-FID）。

Result: 实验结果显示，在视觉保真度和反应级指标上均持续优于此前的听者头部生成方法。

Conclusion: 反应感知的数据、建模与评估对生成自然的听者行为至关重要。

Abstract: While talking head generation has advanced rapidly, generating natural listener behavior in dyadic conversations, which know when to react, how to react, and with what type of response, remains underexplored. Existing dyadic datasets lack fine-grained listener reaction annotations, and prevailing evaluation metrics inherited from talking-head and video generation measure visual realism rather than whether a listener reacted appropriately. We address these gaps along three aspects. First, we curate a listening-head-specific dataset built from RealTalk and Seamless Interaction, comprising approximately 147 hours of paired speaker-listener videos with 64,557 event-level reaction annotations across six categories: nodding, head shaking, smiling, laughing, frowning, and surprised. Second, we introduce an audio-driven baseline built on a flow-matching transformer, namely GLARE, with prosody conditioning derived from Qwen2-Audio and a temporal reaction loss that explicitly supervises frame-wise reactions. Third, we propose a reaction-oriented evaluation protocol that jointly measures reaction occurrence (R-F1), temporal alignment (R-tIoU), asymmetric temporal deviation (R-ATD), and reaction-region visual quality (R-FID), giving a more behaviorally grounded assessment than visual-quality-only metrics. Experiment results show consistent gains over prior listening-head methods in both visual fidelity and reaction-level metrics, suggesting that reaction-aware data, modeling, and evaluation are critical for natural listening behavior.

</details>


### [180] [Atomizer-IO: Beyond Pixels, Patches and Grids](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.40320)
*Hugo Riffaud de Turckheim,Sylvain Lobry,Nicolas Houdré,Damien Robert,Roberto Interdonato,Diego Marcos*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: Atomizer-IO 提出一种"观测优先"的架构，将观测以原子化表示（测量值+采集元数据）并通过局部交叉注意力映射到可任意放置的锚点，从而摆脱规则网格假设，在地球观测与3D点云任务上兼具灵活性与竞争力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多数视觉架构假设观测位于规则网格上，这对自然图像有效，但对通道、时间采样、空间分辨率和几何结构各异的传感数据过于受限；而通用的集合式架构虽去除了网格，却也丢失了有用的空间归纳偏置。作者希望找到一种既能保留空间归纳偏置、又能摆脱网格约束的架构接口。

Method: 提出 Atomizer-IO 架构：基于数据的原子化表示，每个观测由其测量值和采集元数据描述；通过局部交叉注意力将观测映射到可任意放置的锚点，从观测间的物理关系推导结构。通过逐步放松网格假设进行评估：从变化的栅格配置、不完整的通道集合，到灵活的输出密度，最终到无栅格网格的输入。

Result: 在大多数任务上，Atomizer-IO 与灵活的地球观测（EO）专用架构性能相当，同时提供训练后对推理成本的控制，并具有有竞争力的计算量—性能权衡；同一形式无需架构重设计即可扩展到无序3D点云。

Conclusion: 像素、图块和网格无需定义传感架构的接口；原子化的观测优先接口可跨规则栅格与非规则数据（如点云）泛化，为多模态异构传感数据提供统一且灵活的设计范式。

Abstract: Most vision architectures assume that observations lie on a regular grid, an effective abstraction for natural images but a restrictive one for sensing data whose channels, temporal sampling, spatial resolution, and geometry can vary. Generic set-based architectures remove the grid, but also remove useful spatial inductive biases. We introduce Atomizer-IO, an architecture that places observations first and derives structure from their physical relationships. Building on top of an atomic representation of the data, each observation is described by its measurement and acquisition metadata, while local cross-attention maps observations to anchor points that can be arbitrarily placed. We evaluate this design by progressively relaxing the grid assumption, from varying input raster configurations and incomplete channel sets to flexible output density and, ultimately, inputs without a raster grid. Atomizer-IO is competitive with flexible EO-specific architectures on most tasks, while offering post-training control over inference cost and competitive compute--performance trade-offs. The same formulation extends without architectural redesign to unordered 3D point clouds, showing that the atomic interface generalizes beyond regular raster inputs. These results suggest that pixels, patches, and grids do not need to define the interface of a sensing architecture.

</details>


### [181] [MatLoom: Layered Text-to-Material Generation in a Compact Program Space](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.40322)
*Anson Y. Lam,Shuqing Li,Michael R. Lyu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: MatLoom：面向文本到材质生成的紧凑分层程序语言，无需特定微调即可生成可编辑、对齐提示的材质。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统材质生成只产出外观结果，丢失其构建规则，难以复用与后续编辑。作者希望同时生成"外观"与"构造过程"，让依赖关系（图案、颜色、起伏）显式化并可继续创作。

Method: 提出 MatLoom 分层语言：每个程序由带 alpha 蒙版的层组成，共享空间表达式定义覆盖与 PBR 通道；独立解释器将程序求值为材质贴图，源码保留命名字段与层参数。流程无任务专微调，采用解析器引导修复与基于预览的评论来迭代设计，再在固定剩余源码的前提下搜索噪声种子。

Result: 在 141 条提示、六个骨干的基准上，最佳配置在四项平面布局提示对齐指标上均高于三个扩散基线；初始程序在 BLIPScore 均值上已超全部基线（尚未经评论或种子搜索）。跨骨干汇总程序中位长度 21 行。30 名参与者、20 条提示的四方盲测中，其渲染被选率 59.2%，最受偏好基线为 19.3%。

Conclusion: 紧凑可执行程序能在生成提示对齐材质的同时，把"构造方式"保留为资产的一部分。

Abstract: Material generation should produce not only an appearance, but also the rules that construct it. We introduce MatLoom, a compact, layer-oriented language for text-to-material generation with pretrained language models. Each program composes alpha-masked layers whose shared spatial expressions define coverage and physically based rendering (PBR) channels, making dependencies between patterns, color, and relief explicit. A standalone interpreter evaluates the program into material maps, while the source retains named fields and layer parameters for subsequent authoring. Without task-specific fine-tuning, our pipeline uses parser-guided repair and preview-based critique to revise material designs, then searches noise seeds while keeping each candidate's remaining source fixed. On a curated benchmark of 141 prompts evaluated with six backbones, our best-performing configuration achieves higher mean scores than three diffusion baselines on all four flat-layout prompt-alignment metrics. Its initial programs already exceed all three baselines on mean BLIPScore, before critique or seed search. Retained programs have a median length of 21 lines when pooled across backbones. In a blind four-way comparison involving 30 participants and 20 prompts, our renders receive 59.2% of choices, compared with 19.3% for the most-preferred baseline. Compact executable programs thus offer a way to generate prompt-aligned materials while retaining their construction as part of the asset.

</details>


### [182] [I Have a Stream: Making Self-Supervised Learning Work on Continuous Video](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.40333)
*Ivan Martinović,Lukas Knobel,Yuki M. Asano*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 该论文研究从连续视频流中进行自监督学习，构建了95小时的WT++数据集，发现批内高相似性是主要挑战，并提出StreamMAE方法以缩小与i.i.d.预训练的差距。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准自监督学习遵循独立采样和全局打乱的范式，与婴儿连续视觉发展的真实过程差异很大。作者希望探索在严格按时间顺序消费帧、无全局重排和多轮回放的连续视频流场景下，自监督学习是否仍然有效，并找出该场景下的核心难点。

Method: 构建WT++城市步行游览视频数据集（95小时）用于流式预训练，并设计完整的评估套件；对比研究对比学习和蒸馏方法在流式场景下的表现，分析批间相似性与批内相似性的影响；提出StreamMAE，在保留MAE重建目标的基础上，引入流感知正则化和运动偏置裁剪选择来适配流式输入管道。

Result: 对比学习和蒸馏方法在流式场景下表现困难；MAE更鲁棒但仍低于标准i.i.d.预训练。高批间相似性并非性能差距的主因，主要挑战是批内高相似性（批内帧近似重复）。StreamMAE优于流式基线，匹配同视频数据的i.i.d. MAE，与ImageNet预训练MAE相当，并在预训练流从12小时扩展到95小时时呈现正向扩展性。

Conclusion: 从连续视频流进行自监督学习是可行的，关键在于解决批内高相似性。StreamMAE通过流感知正则化和运动偏置裁剪有效缓解该问题，性能可媲美i.i.d.和ImageNet预训练，并随流数据规模增长而提升。

Abstract: Self-supervised learning draws inspiration from infant visual development, yet standard training pipelines bear little resemblance to it: images are independently sampled and globally shuffled across epochs. We study self-supervised learning from continuous video streams, where frames are consumed in temporal order using strict sliding-window batches, without global reshuffling or multi-epoch replay. To this end, we construct WT++, a 95-hour urban walking-tour video dataset for streaming pretraining. Combined with a comprehensive evaluation suite we find that contrastive and distillation-based methods struggle in this setting, while MAE is more robust but still falls short of standard i.i.d. pretraining. We find that high inter-batch similarity, caused by sliding-window consumption across consecutive batches, does not explain this gap. The main challenge is high intra-batch similarity, where frames within each batch are near-duplicates. To mitigate this, we propose StreamMAE, which preserves the core MAE reconstruction objective while adapting the input pipeline with stream-aware regularization and motion-biased crop selection. StreamMAE outperforms streaming baselines, matches i.i.d. MAE trained on the same video data, remains competitive with ImageNet-pretrained MAE, and scales positively as the pretraining stream grows from 12 to 95 hours.

</details>


### [183] [Image Classifiers are Efficient Self-Supervised Video Representation Learners](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.40347)
*Owais Iqbal,Sudipta Sarkar,Shyam Marjit,Omprakash Chakraborty,Anirban Chakraborty,Abir Das*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: VideoMSN 将视频表示为"超级图像",用掩码孪生网络让标准图像 ViT 高效学习视频时空表征,无需 3D 架构或重建解码器,在多个基准上达到 SOTA 且预训练轮数大幅减少。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有视频自监督方法多依赖沉重的 3D 架构或基于重建的自编码器,计算成本高、效率低。作者希望复用标准图像 Vision Transformer 与图像基础模型,实现高效且可迁移的视频时空表征学习。

Method: 提出 VideoMSN 掩码孪生网络框架:把视频采样成帧网格构成"超级图像",从中构建两个视图——空间块掩码视图与时间帧掩码视图,并保证跨帧无信息泄漏;用共享 ViT 编码器并以掩码孪生损失对齐两者嵌入,同时捕获运动与外观线索。方法为无解码器形式,可直接基于预训练的 DINO-v3 与 DeiT-v3 图像编码器进行微调式自监督学习。

Result: 在 Kinetics-400、UCF101 和 HMDB51 上取得最先进性能,同时相比以往视频自监督方法所需视频预训练轮数分别减少最多 32 倍与 160 倍;在低样本分类场景中同样表现强劲,验证了所学表征的可迁移性。

Conclusion: VideoMSN 表明借助图像基础模型与掩码孪生对齐,可免解码器地实现高效视频自监督表征学习,兼顾性能与训练效率,并在标签稀缺情况下具备良好迁移能力。

Abstract: We introduce VideoMSN, a Masked Siamese Network framework for efficient self-supervised spatio-temporal representation learning in videos. Instead of relying on heavy 3D architectures or reconstruction-based autoencoders for learning with unlabeled data, we repurpose standard image Vision Transformers by representing videos as super images which are grids composed of frames sampled from videos. From each super image, we construct two views: one with spatial patch masking and the other with temporal frame masking, ensuring no information leakage across frames. A shared Vision Transformer (ViT) encoder aligns their embeddings using a masked Siamese loss, capturing both motion and appearance cues without reconstruction. Our decoder-free formulation leverages an image foundation model towards efficient video representation learning. Starting from pretrained DINO-v3 and DeiT-v3 image encoders, VideoMSN achieves state-of-the-art performance on Kinetics-400, UCF101, and HMDB51 while requiring up to $32\times$ fewer and $160\times$ fewer video pretraining epochs compared to prior video self-supervised learning methods. Our proposed approach also shows strong performance in low-shot classification, confirming the transferability of the learned representations in a label-scarce scenario. Project Page: https://cvir.github.io/projects/videomsn.

</details>


### [184] [AssemblyWorld: Rethinking 3D Assembly with General-Purpose Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.40353)
*Jiahao Zhang,Yeying Fan,Moitreya Chatterjee,Suhas Lohit,Bernhard Egger,Tim K. Marks,Anoop Cherian,Stephen Gould*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出AssemblyWorld交互式3D装配环境与AssemblyWorldBench基准，评测通用预训练智能体能否通过视觉交互完成装配，发现最强系统部件精度80.9%、整体装配成功率仅59.4%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究通用预训练智能体能否在无需装配专用微调的情况下，仅通过视觉交互完成3D物体装配，并填补缺乏统一评测环境的空白。

Method: 构建AssemblyWorld交互式3D环境：智能体只能通过2D渲染视图感知零件几何，操作刚性零件，并可参考图像或装配手册；结果以几何方式评估。基于此构建含100个任务、80个物体（家具、工业装配、断裂重组）的AssemblyWorldBench，并对8个智能体系统进行评测。

Result: 系统间能力差异显著：最强系统部件精度80.9%，完整装配成功率59.4%；开源系统在执行可靠性与装配精度上明显落后于闭源系统。对视觉参考、交互轨迹与失败案例的分析显示，智能体能修正装配但仍残留定位误差。

Conclusion: AssemblyWorld为评估交互式装配智能体提供了统一平台，可用于刻画"近似结构恢复"与"精确重建"之间的能力差距。

Abstract: The task of 3D assembly requires translating an understanding of parts and their relationships into precise spatial arrangements. Can pretrained general-purpose agents assemble objects through visual interaction without additional assembly-specific fine-tuning? To investigate this question, we introduce AssemblyWorld, an interactive 3D environment in which agents inspect rendered views and manipulate supplied rigid parts, guided by images or assembly manuals when available. Agents perceive part geometry through 2D views rather than direct access to mesh vertices or faces, while their resulting assemblies are evaluated geometrically. Building on this environment, we construct AssemblyWorldBench, comprising 100 assembly tasks across 80 objects spanning furniture, industrial assembly, and fracture reassembly. Evaluating eight agent systems reveals substantial differences in their capabilities. The strongest system achieves 80.9% part accuracy but 59.4% complete-assembly success. The evaluated open-source systems lag substantially behind their stronger closed-source peers in both execution reliability and assembly accuracy. Analyses of visual references, interaction trajectories, and failures show how agents revise assemblies while leaving residual positioning errors. AssemblyWorld provides a common setting for both assessing the capabilities of interactive assembly agents and characterizing the gap between approximate structure recovery and precise reconstruction.

</details>


### [185] [ViTeX-Bench: Benchmarking High-Fidelity Video Scene Text Editing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.40356)
*Xinghao Chen,Xiangbo Gao,Jiongze Yu,Yuheng Wu,Zhengzhong Tu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 该论文提出 ViTeX-Bench 基准套件（含 ViTeX-Dataset 与三轴评估协议）以及开源编辑器 ViTeX-Edit-14B，用于研究视频场景文本编辑中的文本准确性、时序一致性与编辑局部性之间的权衡。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 视频生成日益逼真可控，但视频编辑仍较落后，尤其是需保留原始场景动态的精确局部编辑。视频场景文本编辑（如店面招牌、白板、产品标签上的文字替换）虽在图像领域研究成熟，但视频领域在高视觉质量、时序一致性与编辑局部性方面仍探索不足。现有资源缺乏配对真实视频数据，通用视频编辑指标也无法直接衡量目标文本在时间上是否保持正确。

Method: 方法包括三部分：（1）构建 ViTeX-Dataset，包含 387 个真实 720p 视频，附带文本区域掩码与编辑指令，其中 230 个为经审核、流程生成的配对编辑样本用于训练，157 个作为冻结评估集；（2）设计三轴评估协议，通过 13 项指标评估文本正确性、视觉与时序质量、编辑局部性，每轴一个主指标并进行 Pareto 权衡比较，辅以 OCR 校准、人工评估与标注敏感性分析；（3）发布 ViTeX-Edit-14B 参考编辑器，在配对训练集上微调，采用运动对齐的字形-视频条件化。

Result: 在来自四个编辑家族的八个基线方法上，准确文本、时序稳定性与场景保持难以同时实现。ViTeX-Edit-14B 取得 CharAcc 0.688，为所评估视频原生编辑器中最高均值，并在原始编辑器输出中拥有最低的可比文本裁剪 Warp。

Conclusion: ViTeX-Bench 为研究视频场景文本编辑中的上述权衡提供了可复现的基础。

Abstract: Recent video generation is increasingly realistic and controllable, yet video editing remains less developed, particularly for precise local edits that must preserve the original scene dynamics. Video scene text editing replaces text on scene surfaces, such as storefront signs, whiteboards, and product labels, while preserving the surrounding content, motion, and camera dynamics. Although scene text editing is well studied for images, video scene text editing that achieves high visual quality, temporal consistency, and edit locality remains underexplored. Existing resources offer limited paired real-video data, and general video-editing metrics do not directly measure whether the requested text remains correct over time. We introduce ViTeX-Bench, a benchmark suite comprising ViTeX-Dataset and a three-axis evaluation protocol. The dataset contains 387 real-world 720p videos with text-region masks and editing instructions: 230 provide reviewed, pipeline-generated paired edits for training, and 157 form a frozen evaluation split. The protocol evaluates text correctness, visual and temporal quality, and edit locality through 13 metrics, with one primary metric per axis and a Pareto comparison of their trade-offs. OCR calibration, human evaluation, and annotation-sensitivity analyses support the interpretation of these scores. Across eight baselines from four editing families, accurate text, temporal stability, and scene preservation remain difficult to achieve together. We also release ViTeX-Edit-14B, an open-source reference editor fine-tuned on the paired training split with motion-aligned glyph-video conditioning. It achieves CharAcc 0.688, the highest mean among the evaluated video-native editors, and the lowest comparable text-crop Warp among raw editor outputs. ViTeX-Bench provides a reproducible foundation for studying these trade-offs in video scene text editing.

</details>


### [186] [Physis-Lang: Self-Evolving Language as a Physical Representation for Video World Model](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.40358)
*Liming Lu,Xianzheng Ma,Wenkun He,Guanqi Zhan,Yilin Zhao,Junyu Chen,Mengyao Xu,Jiaojiao Fan,Wenhang Ge,Yuchao Gu,Yunze Liu,Boyi Li,Zhen Dong,Victor Prisacariu,Ming-Yu Liu,Song Han,Han Cai*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 论文提出 Physis-Lang，将物理语言作为数据筛选、模型训练与视频生成中共享且可优化的表示，无需引入视觉、数值或规划等额外信号，即可提升视频世界模型的物理合理性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有视频世界模型常生成视觉逼真但违背物理规律的视频。多数方法认为自然语言不足以表达可靠的物理知识，因而引入额外的视觉、隐空间、数值或规划信号。本文重新审视这一假设，探索仅用语言表示物理知识是否足够。

Method: 将物理过程用语言描述为实体、成因、交互、支配原理、时间演化与效应；构建 PhysCapBench，把物理过程分解为原子断言并用召回率与精确率评估描述；通过智能体循环迭代分析断言级错误并优化生成物理描述的指令；将模型缺陷转化为文本描述，并用语言引导检索找到覆盖缺失物理过程的视觉多样视频。

Result: 在 Wan 与 Cosmos 骨干上的四个常用物理视频基准上，物理合理性一致提升；从开源 Cosmos3-Nano 出发，经 Physis-Lang 增强的模型超过领先的专有模型 Veo 3.1。

Conclusion: 自然语言可作为一种共享且可优化的物理知识表示，贯穿数据、训练与生成全流程，有效提升视频世界模型的物理合理性，并具备超越专有模型的潜力。

Abstract: Video world models are expected to predict how the physical world evolves, yet they often produce visually plausible videos that violate basic physical principles. Existing approaches commonly assume that natural language is insufficient to represent the physical knowledge required for reliable generation, and therefore introduce additional visual, latent, numerical, or planning-based signals. We revisit this assumption and introduce Physis-Lang, a self-evolving framework that treats physical language as a shared and optimizable representation across data curation, model training, and video generation. Physis-Lang represents physical processes through language that describes their relevant entities, causes, interactions, governing principles, temporal evolution, and effects. To improve this representation, we construct PhysCapBench, which decomposes physical processes into atomic assertions and evaluates captions using recall and precision. An agentic loop iteratively analyzes assertion-level errors and refines the instruction used to produce physical captions. Physis-Lang further converts model deficiencies into textual descriptions and uses language-guided retrieval to identify visually diverse videos that cover missing physical processes. Experiments on four widely used physical video benchmarks with Wan and Cosmos backbones demonstrate consistent improvements in physical plausibility. Notably, starting from open-source Cosmos3-Nano backbones, our Physis-Lang-enhanced models surpass the leading proprietary Veo 3.1 model.

</details>


### [187] [Multimodal Flow: Unified Flow Modeling of Language and Vision in Embedding Spaces](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.40362)
*Hongyuan Tao,Xinggang Wang,Lianghui Zhu,Yongkang Li,Yunchao Wei,Bin Feng,Shaoyu Chen,Qian Zhang,Chang Huang,Kai Yu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出 Multimodal Flow，一种完全连续的多模态生成模型，用统一的连续表示与共享的 chunk-causal flow backbone 同时建模语言与视觉，避免离散量化瓶颈与模态依赖目标，在多规模上取得有竞争力的统一多模态建模效果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有统一多模态模型主要有两类：一是把语言和量化图像都建模为离散 token，会引入视觉量化瓶颈；二是离散语言预测与连续图像生成相组合，需要模态依赖的目标函数与采样流程。完全连续建模可同时避开这两类取舍并共享生成过程，但在多模态预训练中尚缺探索。

Method: Multimodal Flow 整合多模态连续表示与共享的 chunk-causal flow backbone。将文本块与图像组织为有序连续 hyperchunks，保留文本 token 顺序与视觉空间结构。Backbone 通过 Flow Matching 在这些 hyperchunks 上学习单一向量场。联合注意力实现跨模态交互，模态专属前馈网络分别处理各模态。训练时并行预测多个目标 chunk，推理时顺序生成 hyperchunks。实例化为 MF-1 并在多模态数据上预训练。

Result: 在 0.6B、1.2B、1.6B 规模上，继续预训练持续提升多模态建模。仅用 150B 预训练 token，MF-1 在 GenEval 与 DPG-Bench 上平均得分 82.8，在 VQAv2、MMBench、POPE 上平均 75.3，与使用显著更多数据训练的统合模型相比仍具竞争力。在匹配数据、优化与参数预算下，Multimodal Flow 优于代表性的混合模型与离散模型。

Conclusion: 结果确立了连续 chunk-based embedding flow 建模作为一种新的完全连续统一多模态建模范式；相关代码与模型已公开（https://github.com/hustvl/Multimodal-Flow）。

Abstract: We present Multimodal Flow, a fully continuous generative model of language and vision. Most unified multimodal models either model both language and quantized images as discrete tokens or combine discrete language prediction with continuous image generation. The former introduces a visual quantization bottleneck. The latter requires modality-dependent objectives and sampling procedures. Fully continuous modeling avoids these trade-offs and enables a shared generative process, but remains underexplored for multimodal pretraining. Multimodal Flow introduces a unified continuous architecture that integrates multimodal continuous representations with a shared chunk-causal flow backbone. It organizes text blocks and images as ordered continuous hyperchunks, preserving textual token order and visual spatial structure. The backbone learns a single vector field over these hyperchunks through Flow Matching. Joint attention enables cross-modal interaction, while modality-specific feed-forward networks process each modality. The model predicts multiple target chunks in parallel during training and generates hyperchunks sequentially at inference. We instantiate MF-1 and pretrain it on multimodal data. Across 0.6B, 1.2B, and 1.6B scales, continued pretraining consistently improves multimodal modeling. With only 150B pretraining tokens, MF-1 achieves an average score of 82.8 across GenEval and DPG-Bench and 75.3 across VQAv2, MMBench, and POPE, remaining competitive with unified models trained on substantially more data. Under matched data, optimization, and parameter budgets, Multimodal Flow further outperforms representative hybrid and discrete models. These results establish continuous chunk-based embedding flow modeling as a new fully continuous paradigm for unified multimodal modeling. The related code and model are publicly released at https://github.com/hustvl/Multimodal-Flow.

</details>
